I don't think so. I definitely haven't felt unusually energized or anything like that. Just... no.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had times when you felt so good or so hyper that people around you noticed a change in your behavior or thought you were acting unusually?\n\nPatient: No. I... I'm not really a hyper person. Nobody has ever pointed out anything like that to me.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever experienced any memory gaps, or times when you felt detached from yourself or as if you were watching yourself from outside your body?\n\nPatient: No... I remember things fine. I haven't ever felt like I was outside my body or anything. Just... no, I haven't had that.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt like you're not all 'you,' or like there are different parts of you that act or think differently, almost as if there are multiple people inside you?\n\nPatient: No. I'm just... me. I don't feel like there are other people in my head.\n\nDoctor: Have you experienced any particularly stressful or traumatic events recently before these behaviors started?\n\nPatient: Yeah... I don't think so. I know who I am. I haven't felt anything like that.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt like you were losing your sense of who you are, or like you were watching yourself act as if you were a different person, even for a short moment?\n\nPatient: No...
